Abstract: Reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF) has emerged as a powerful yet sample-inefficient approach for learning reward models from human preferences, making active learning a critical component in synthesizing informative preference queries. However, effective uncertainty quantification required for active learning remains a key challenge for large neural network reward models. In this paper, we introduce PreferenceEKF, a sample-efficient approach that tracks reward model uncertainty by framing active preference learning as a sequential Bayesian filtering problem. Instead of relying on computationally prohibitive posterior inference over the full neural network parameter space, our method performs sequential inference via an extended Kalman filter within a low-dimensional parameter subspace, continuously updating the reward model posterior as new preference queries arrive. Our approach enables scalable sampling of neural network parameters to efficiently compute acquisition functions for active reward learning. Experiments on the D4RL and V-D4RL benchmarks demonstrate that our approach achieves better sample efficiency, runtime, scalability, and calibration compared to other Bayesian deep learning approaches, and the learned reward models lead to competitive offline reinforcement learning policy performance. This highlights the potential of scalable Bayesian methods for preference-based reward modeling in RLHF. Our code is available at https://github.com/yutaizhou/bnn_pref.

</details>


### [259] [Conditioning Degenerate Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04090)
*Uğur Aydın,Tamer Başar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对条件生成模型中当扩散过程具有奇异扩散系数、且底层条件密度不存在或不光滑时，传统基于分数（score）函数的引导训练失效的问题，提出利用因果最优传输来构造近似损失函数，从而在极弱假设下识别用于引导的最小熵控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有条件生成模型在训练与引导过程中高度依赖分数函数；然而当扩散系数奇异、底层条件密度不存在或不光滑时，分数函数不再良定义，导致这类方法失效。因此需要一种不依赖密度光滑性的替代指导框架。

Method: 借助因果最优传输（causal optimal transport）定义近似损失函数，并利用（条件）扩散过程的可预测表示性质——其关联的鞅问题良定（à la Üstünel）——对损失函数进行刻画，从而在最小假设下识别出一个最小熵控制用于引导。

Result: 在扩散系数奇异且条件密度不存在或非光滑的退化情形下，仍然能够构造出良定义的近似引导目标，并识别出相应的最小熵控制，实现了对分数引导方法的推广。

Conclusion: 文章表明，因果最优传输为条件生成模型的引导提供了超越传统分数方法的一般性理论基础，使适用范围扩展到密度不存在或不光滑的奇异扩散场景，同时只需鞅问题良定等最小化假设。

Abstract: Current conditioned generative models heavily rely on score functions for guidance during training. When the generative model is a diffusion process with a singular diffusion coefficient and the underlying (conditional) densities either do not exist or are not smooth, we use causal optimal transport to define \emph{approximate} loss functions that identify a minimum-entropy control for guidance under minimal assumptions. Our approach relies on causal optimal transport and its characterization through the predictable representation property of (conditioned) diffusion processes whose associated martingale problem is well posed, à la Üstünel.

</details>


### [260] [Hardware-Aware FP4 FlashAttention-4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04105)
*Robert Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Direct-P方法，解决Blackwell FP4张量核心上注意力因softmax转换和片上依赖而无法加速的问题：非因果推理直接将分数映射为FP4概率，因果路径将前向量化直接传入反向传播并用FP8梯度操作数，在GB200上分别实现最高2.13倍BF16前向吞吐和1.14倍完整单GPU 8B参数更新加速；同时发现分布式训练中MXFP4概率/值轨迹全部发散，必须保留FP8。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Blackwell的4位浮点(FP4)张量核心并不能自动使注意力更快，因为一旦矩阵乘积缩小，softmax转换和片上依赖成为瓶颈；此外需要验证FP4量化在注意力及训练中的可行性。

Method: 提出Direct-P：对非因果推理，将注意力分数直接映射为FP4概率；对因果路径，将前向量化直接传入反向传播，从保存的量化查询和键重建概率，并使用FP8梯度操作数；在分布式训练中匹配FP8概率和值。

Result: Direct-P在NVIDIA GB200上非因果前向吞吐最高达BF16的2.13倍；因果路径使完整单GPU 8B参数更新加速最高达1.14倍；所有测试的MXFP4概率/值训练轨迹均发散，而匹配FP8概率和值的分布式训练可保持稳定。

Conclusion: 通过直接FP4概率映射和将量化传入反向传播，可使FP4张量核心在注意力中实现实际加速；但训练中概率和值必须保留FP8精度，MXFP4概率/值方案不可行。

Abstract: Blackwell's 4-bit floating-point (FP4) tensor cores do not automatically make attention faster because softmax conversion and on-chip dependencies dominate once its matrix products shrink. We address this with \emph{Direct-P} for noncausal inference and a causal path that passes the forward quantization directly into backward. Direct-P maps scores directly to FP4 probabilities and reaches up to 2.13$\times$ the bfloat16 (BF16) forward throughput on an NVIDIA GB200. The causal path reconstructs probabilities from saved quantized queries and keys and uses 8-bit floating-point (FP8) gradient operands, accelerating a complete single-GPU 8-billion-parameter update by up to 1.14$\times$. Matched distributed training retains FP8 probabilities and values; every tested MXFP4 probability/value training trajectory diverges.

</details>


### [261] [Prospective Coding Improves Learning in Deep Continuous-Time Recurrent Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04134)
*Shivang Rawat,Mirko Morello,Flaviano Morone,David J. Heeger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出递归正交滤波器（RQF），一种受生物启发的复值时间滤波器（对角状态空间模型的特例），并提出"前瞻性输入编码"（prospective-input coding）这一输入侧修正方法，以缓解深度连续时间循环网络中层间信号延迟与梯度衰减问题。实验表明，在前瞻性变体下，RQF、S5和ORGaNICs在完整BPTT与空间-only反传下均匹配或超越基线，且RQF在Speech Commands上以31.9k参数达到96.09%准确率，在Path-X任务上达到83.56%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续时间循环网络中的时间积分赋予网络记忆能力，但在深层堆叠中，这种积分会延迟自底向上的输入信号并削弱自顶向下的误差信号，导致深度相关的性能退化。作者希望探究能否通过使每一层的自底向上输入变得"前瞻"（prospective）来解决这一失效模式。

Method: 首先从能量模型出发推导RQF动力学，证明每个RQF是一个带通滤波器，其可学习参数控制调谐频率和带宽。随后，利用一个无参数的"两抽头"（two-tap）更新使每层自底向上输入前瞻化，该更新不改变循环迁移和并行扫描；并将该修正扩展到一般对角SSM，证明其能在时间梯度被截断（即空间-only反传）时缓解随深度增加的梯度衰减。评估中分别在RQF、S5和ORGaNICs（非线性门控RNN）上使用完整BPTT和空间-only反传进行训练。

Result: 在完整BPTT下，前瞻性变体在所有模型与配置中均匹配或超过非前瞻对照组。非残差宽度32的六层RQF在raw-audio Speech Commands上以31.9k参数达到96.09%准确率；宽度64的六层RQF在16,384步的Path-X任务上达到83.56%。

Conclusion: RQF是一种参数高效的循环基底；前瞻性输入编码是一种针对深度连续时间循环网络的有效输入侧修正方法，能够在不改动循环动力学的前提下缓解深度带来的信号延迟与误差衰减问题，并在多个任务上取得领先性能。

Abstract: Temporal integration gives continuous-time recurrent networks memory, but in deep stacks it also delays bottom-up signals and attenuates top-down errors. We develop Recursive Quadrature Filters (RQFs), biologically motivated complex-valued temporal filters that are a special case of diagonal state-space models (SSMs), and ask whether this failure mode can be addressed by making each layer's bottom-up input prospective. Starting from an energy model, we derive the RQF dynamics and show that each RQF is a band-pass filter whose learnable parameters control its tuning frequency and bandwidth. We then make each layer's bottom-up input prospective using a parameter-free two-tap update that leaves the recurrent transition and parallel scan unchanged. We extend this correction to general diagonal SSMs and show that it mitigates depth-dependent gradient attenuation when temporal gradients are truncated, i.e., spatial-only backpropagation. We evaluate the intervention in RQFs, S5, and ORGaNICs (a nonlinear gated RNN) trained using full backpropagation through time (BPTT) and spatial-only backpropagation. Under full BPTT, prospective variants match or outperform their non-prospective controls in every model and configuration. A non-residual width-32 six-layer RQF reaches 96.09% accuracy on raw-audio Speech Commands with 31.9k parameters; a width-64 six-layer RQF reaches 83.56% on the 16,384-step Path-X task. These results identify RQFs as a parameter-efficient recurrent substrate and prospective-input coding as an input-side correction for deep continuous-time recurrent networks.

</details>


### [262] [A Low-Cost, Open Platform for End-to-End Autonomous Driving on a Miniature Ackermann Vehicle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04147)
*Gustavo Claudio Karl Couto,Eric Aislan Antonelo,Gabriel George Zipperer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个低成本的开放式微型Ackermann车辆端到端自动驾驶实验平台，包含实体车辆、印刷城市赛道、数据采集工具、轨迹注册和Webots数字孪生，并提供基于命令条件行为克隆的基线方法。实验表明该策略在实体车上可达6.1 cm的平均横向误差，数字孪生中加宽相机视场可将误差从35.6 cm降至3.3 cm，且结合合成数据与真实数据训练的高容量策略是唯一能在闭环中完成全部四条赛道配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端自动驾驶研究常在仿真与真实世界之间存在较大差距，缺乏低成本、可复现、能连接仿真方法与实体执行的实验平台，限制了sim-to-real研究的可控实验与对照验证。

Method: 构建集实体微型车、印刷赛道、数据采集、轨迹注册与Webots数字孪生于一体的开源平台；以命令条件行为克隆为基线，神经策略输入车载相机图像与高层导航命令，输出转向与速度；并利用数字孪生生成合成数据，结合学习的sim-to-real图像翻译器缩小外观差异，训练更高容量策略。

Result: 实体闭环实验中，策略可循道行驶并执行转弯命令，平均横向误差6.1 cm，逼近人工演示的4.7 cm；数字孪生中相机视场从58度加宽到120度时，平均横向误差由35.6 cm降至3.3 cm；仅由合成数据+真实演示训练的高容量策略能在闭环中完成全部四条赛道，而紧凑基线与仅用真实数据训练的相同网络完成赛道数更少。

Conclusion: 该开放式平台可作为实用的sim-to-real研究测试平台，并提供了初始的命令条件模仿学习基线；平台已开源以支持可复现研究。

Abstract: This paper presents a low-cost, open experimental platform for research in end-to-end autonomous driving with miniature Ackermann vehicles. The platform combines a physical vehicle, a printed urban track, data collection tools, trajectory registration, and a Webots digital twin, enabling controlled experiments that connect simulation-based autonomous-driving methods to real-world execution. As a first baseline, we implement command-conditioned behavior cloning, in which a neural policy receives an on-board camera image and a high-level navigation command and outputs steering and speed. The system is evaluated both on the physical vehicle and in simulation. In real closed-loop experiments, the learned policy follows lanes and executes commanded turns, reaching a mean cross-track error of 6.1 cm with respect to the reference route, close to the 4.7 cm observed in human demonstrations. In the digital twin, camera field of view has a strong effect on performance, reducing the mean cross-track error from 35.6 to 3.3 cm when widened from 58 to 120 degrees. Using the digital twin to generate synthetic driving data and a learned sim-to-real image translator to reduce the appearance gap, we further show that a higher-capacity policy trained on this synthetic data combined with real demonstrations is the only configuration that completes all four track routes in closed loop, whereas the compact baseline and the same network trained on real data alone complete fewer. These results establish the open platform as a practical testbed for sim-to-real studies and provide an initial command-conditioned imitation-learning baseline; we release it to support reproducible research.

</details>


<div id='cs.GT'></div>

# cs.GT [[Back]](#toc)

### [263] [Turn-Based Combat Arena: A New Framework for Multiagent Training and Game Balancing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03122)
*V. M. Vasyuta,V. V. Malitskyi,O. S. Kushnir,B. I. Horon,V. A. Franiv*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个用于回合制策略游戏的模块化框架（Turn-Based Combat Arena），支持高效训练和评估机器学习智能体，并重点研究了游戏平衡（特别是单位参数）的优化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回合制策略游戏中机器学习智能体的训练与评估需要高效、可配置且可扩展的实验平台；同时游戏平衡（尤其是单位参数）的自动调优缺乏系统性的研究支撑。

Method: 提出可配置的回合制战斗框架，支持灵活修改游戏规则与参数以进行快速多样化实验；采用高吞吐量架构以支持每秒数万局模拟及单机数十亿条对局记录的处理；并评估多种优化方法用于游戏单位参数的平衡。

Result: 多种优化方法收敛到可比较的解决方案，表明在寻找平衡游戏配置方面具有鲁棒性；框架在高吞吐模拟与大规模数据存储方面表现良好。

Conclusion: 该框架可作为一种实用的平台，同时服务于游戏设计分析与智能体训练。

Abstract: This paper is the first in a series on Turn-Based Combat Arena, a configurable framework for turn-based strategy games designed to support the efficient training and evaluation of machine learning agents. The proposed framework enables flexible modification of game rules and parameters, allowing rapid experimentation across diverse scenarios. Its architecture is optimized for high-throughput simulation, supporting tens of thousands of games per second and enabling the storage and processing of billions of gameplay records on a single machine. The problem of balancing the game, and particularly the parameters of game units, is investigated in detail. We evaluate several optimization approaches and show that multiple methods converge to comparable solutions, suggesting robustness in identifying balanced game configurations. These results indicate that the framework can serve as a practical platform for both game design analysis and agent training.

</details>


### [264] [Otter: A Provably MEV-Resilient Automated Market Maker via Surplus Redistribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03474)
*Elaine Shi,Mengqian Zhang,Hao Chung,Yuhao Li*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文提出 Otter（Optimal Truthful Trading with Excess Redistribution），一种具有可证明 MEV 抗性的双资产批量 AMM。在共识层具备审查抗性且区块空间不拥塞时，Otter 使诚实行为成为用户与构建者的占优策略，并利用"剩余盈余再分配"范式将残余盈余导向社区而非被 MEV 捕获，同时在满足博弈论性质的自然机制类中最大化社会福利。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AMM 中的矿工可提取价值（MEV）使区块构建者能够通过交易排序和注入交易获利，这给用户带来成本并加剧构建者中心化，因此需要一种能够消除 MEV 攫取动机的机制设计。

Method: 提出 Otter 双资产批量 AMM，其关键创新是"剩余盈余再分配"（surplus redistribution）范式：批次中未分配完的输出代币不必须全部分给当前用户，而可转入由去中心化社区治理的智能合约，用于补贴交易费、奖励流动性提供者或回流资金池。该机制依赖共识层提供审查抗性，并辅以不可能性结果证明：若允许构建者审查交易，则所需博弈论保证不可实现。

Result: Otter 使得诚实行为对用户和构建者均为占优策略；即使构建者本身是拥有内在价值的交易者，也无法通过重排投标或注入女巫投标获利。同时，Otter 在满足所需博弈论性质的自然机制类中，以强意义下最大化社会福利。

Conclusion: 该工作为应用层的 MEV 抗性提供了数学上正式的证据：共识层的安全保证（如审查抗性）能够从根本上扩展应用层可实现的设计空间；缺失审查抗性时，期望的博弈论保证无法达成。

Abstract: Miner extractable value (MEV) in automated market makers allows block builders to profit from transaction ordering and injected trades, imposing costs on users and contributing to builder centralization. We introduce Otter (Optimal Truthful Trading with Excess Redistribution), a two-asset batch AMM that achieves provable MEV resilience when the consensus layer provides censorship resilience and block space is uncongested. Otter makes truthful behavior a dominant strategy for both users and builders. Consequently, a builder cannot profit from strategic deviations, including reordering bids or injecting sybil bids, even when the builder is itself a trader with intrinsic value. Moreover, we show that Otter maximizes social welfare, in a strong sense, within a natural class of mechanisms satisfying the desired game-theoretic properties.
  To achieve these guarantees, we introduce a new paradigm called surplus redistribution, which provably prevents residual surplus from being captured as MEV by redirecting it to the broader community. Specifically, the pool's output tokens need not be distributed entirely among users in the current batch. Instead, residual surplus may be transferred to a smart contract governed by the decentralized community. The accumulated surplus can be used to benefit community members --- for example, by subsidizing transaction fees, rewarding liquidity providers, or returning assets to the pool.
  Our approach relies on the underlying consensus layer to provide censorship resilience. We motivate its necessity through an impossibility result showing that the desired game-theoretic guarantees become unattainable when the builder is additionally allowed to censor transactions. Thus, our results also provide a mathematically formal demonstration of how consensus-level security guarantees can fundamentally expand what is achievable at the application layer.

</details>


### [265] [EF1-Constrained Nash Social Welfare with Identical Additive Valuations: Complexity, Guarantees, and Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03846)
*Zih-Sian Yang,Yi-Hao Chen,Yu-Te Kuan,Cheng-Jui Wu,Chuang-Chieh Lin,Po-An Chen*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究同质可加性估价下不可分割商品分配的EF1（至多一件商品的无嫉妒）与纳什社会福利（NSW）问题。理论上，由于最大NSW分配必为EF1且NSW最大化是强NP难问题，作者转而刻画任意EF1分配的福利保证，给出了均匀估价下EF1分配即NSW最优、以及ε-小件条件下EF1分配达到1-O(ε²)近似比的结论；工程上，提出基于PPO的深度强化学习框架PriorityNet，通过前瞻性EF1动作掩码在每步分配中保持EF1，在离线与随机序在线场景下分别达到0.9911和0.9701的归一化NSW，优于LPT与在线最小价值束基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在不可分割商品分配中，EF1是公平性的核心概念。已知最大NSW分配在可加性估价下必为EF1，故EF1阈值问题继承了NSW最大化的强NP完全性，难以精确求解。因此作者希望：（1）从理论上刻画任意EF1分配（不限于NSW最优）所能保证的福利下界，找出能给出更强保证的条件；（2）在实际算法层面，在维持EF1这一硬性约束的同时追求高NSW，尤其是考虑"每步分配后都保持EF1"的更强的序贯要求。

Method: 理论上采用组合刻画：证明在均匀估价下，任何EF1分配的NSW即为最优；在ε-小件条件（所有商品价值相对于整体足够小）下，推导任意EF1分配满足的显式近似比ρ_n(ε)，并证明当ε→0时ρ_n(ε)=1-O(ε²)（对固定n）。算法上提出PriorityNet：用深度强化学习（近端策略优化PPO）训练智能体，并配备前瞻性EF1动作掩码——在每一步决策时仅允许选择能维持EF1的分配，从而在构造上保证前缀EF1（prefix-wise EF1），无需事后修复。

Result: 在n∈[2,20]、m∈[5,100]的3000个测试实例上，PriorityNet在离线与随机序在线两种场景下分别达到平均归一化NSW 0.9911和0.9701；与离线最长处理时间（LPT）和在线最小价值束基线相比，逐实例胜负差率分别为+27.10%和+17.87%；离线平均归一化福利与基线持平（四位小数一致），在线均值从0.9694提升到0.9701。

Conclusion: 理论上，任意EF1分配在均匀估价下实现NSW最优，在ε-小件条件下达到1-O(ε²)的近似保证，说明EF1分配具有很强的内在福利边界；工程上，将EF1作为硬约束嵌入深度强化学习（通过前瞻性动作掩码）可同时保证前缀EF1与接近最优的NSW，验证了在公平约束下通过学习驱动方式获得高性能分配的可行性。

Abstract: We study the allocation of indivisible goods among agents with identical additive valuations, focusing on envy-freeness up to one good (EF1) and Nash social welfare (NSW). Since every maximum-NSW allocation is EF1 under additive valuations, the associated threshold problem inherits the known strong NP-hardness of NSW maximization under identical additive valuations and is strongly NP-complete. We therefore focus on welfare guarantees satisfied by arbitrary EF1 allocations. Although every such allocation is known to achieve an $e^{-1/e}$-approximation to the unrestricted optimal NSW, we identify conditions yielding stronger guarantees. Under uniform valuations, every EF1 allocation is NSW-optimal. Under an $\varepsilon$-small-item condition, every EF1 allocation achieves an explicit approximation ratio $ρ_n(\varepsilon)$ satisfying $ρ_n(\varepsilon) = 1-O(\varepsilon^2)$ as $\varepsilon\to 0$ for fixed $n$.
  We further consider the stronger sequential requirement that EF1 be maintained after every item assignment. For this setting, we propose \emph{PriorityNet}, a deep reinforcement learning framework trained using Proximal Policy Optimization and equipped with prospective EF1 action masking. The mask restricts every decision to assignments that preserve EF1, thereby guaranteeing prefix-wise EF1 by construction without post-processing repair. Across 3,000 test instances in each of the offline and random-order online regimes ($n\in[2,20]$ and $m\in[5,100]$), PriorityNet attains mean normalized $\operatorname{NSW}$ values of $0.9911$ and $0.9701$, respectively. Relative to offline Longest Processing Time (LPT) and online least-valued-bundle baselines, it achieves instance-wise win-minus-loss rates of $+27.10\%$ and $+17.87\%$, while matching the offline baseline's mean normalized welfare to four decimal places and modestly improving the online mean from $0.9694$ to $0.9701$.

</details>


<div id='cs.CE'></div>

# cs.CE [[Back]](#toc)

### [266] [Markovian Shock-Source Tracing and Multidimensional Asset Roles in Exchange Rates, Gold Futures, and Bitcoin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03437)
*Seung Ho Choi,Seoin Jang,Hyunwoo Lee,Hayoung Choi*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文研究国际金融网络中主要汇率、黄金期货与比特币之间的跨资产溢出连通性，在传统净发射器-接收器分类之外，提出直接溢出传输、平稳源追踪动态和多步上游连通性三个互补维度，并基于VAR-GFEVD与马尔可夫核方法刻画资产角色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的净发射器-接收器分类无法充分刻画金融网络中资产角色的复杂性，尤其是间接、多步和长期的上游连通特征可能被净溢出指标掩盖。本文旨在超越单一净溢出视角，从多个互补维度更全面地刻画资产在网络中的角色。

Method: 采用VAR广义预测误差方差分解（VAR-GFEVD）估计收益溢出；将正的成对净溢出结构映射为行随机的马尔可夫核，其转移过程沿原始传播边的反向追踪主导净溢出源；利用平稳离开通量刻画源追踪链的长期运动；并采用Fink等人的确定性概率传播算法计算Viral Centrality以近似多步上游可达性。

Result: 实证结果显示，黄金期货是主导的直接净发射器且是领先的平稳源追踪节点，但其Viral Centrality并非最大；若干被归类为直接净接收器的汇率节点具有较高的Viral Centrality，表明其对上游源节点具有广泛的条件访问路径；比特币处于中间角色。加入美元指数（USD Index）的辅助设定得到性质相似的角色分化结果。

Conclusion: 直接连通性、平稳源追踪与近似多步上游可达性无法仅由净溢出指标推断，三者刻画了网络角色中截然不同的维度。所有扩散解释均为描述性的，且依赖于估计网络结构、行归一化及重启闭合等设定条件。

Abstract: This study examines cross-asset connectedness in an international financial network of major exchange rates, gold futures, and Bitcoin. Moving beyond the conventional net transmitter--receiver classification, we characterize asset roles through three complementary dimensions: direct spillover transmission, stationary source-tracing dynamics, and multistep upstream connectivity. Return spillovers are estimated using VAR generalized forecast-error variance decomposition (VAR-GFEVD). The positive pairwise net-spillover structure is then mapped into a row-stochastic Markov kernel whose transitions trace dominant net-spillover sources in the reverse direction of the original transmission edges. A stationary departure flux describes long-run movement in this source-tracing chain, while Viral Centrality is evaluated by the deterministic probability-propagation algorithm of Fink et al. to approximate multistep upstream reach. Empirically, gold futures emerge as the dominant direct net transmitter and the leading stationary source-tracing node, but do not have the largest Viral Centrality. Several exchange-rate nodes classified as direct net receivers have relatively high Viral Centrality, indicating broad conditional access to upstream source nodes. Bitcoin occupies an intermediate role. An auxiliary specification including the U.S. Dollar Index yields qualitatively similar role differentiation. The results show that direct connectedness, stationary source tracing, and approximate multistep upstream reach cannot be inferred from NET spillovers alone. All diffusion interpretations are descriptive and conditional on the estimated network, row normalization, and restart closure.

</details>


### [267] [Calibration of neural viscoelastic models via full-field data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03645)
*Brain M. Riemer,Markus Kästner,Karl A. Kalina*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 提出了一种无监督学习框架，利用全场数据（全局反作用力与表面位移）标定用于小应变黏弹性特性的物理增强神经网络（PANN）。该网络嵌入广义标准材料（GSM）理论，通过两个标量势函数确保热力学一致性，并采用基于不变量的表示保证材料对称性。在平面应力假设下，结合平衡间隙法、拟牛顿优化器与自动微分构建约束优化问题；隐式时间积分处理内变量演化，局部牛顿迭代求解非线性方程组，并用伴随（adjoint）方法高效计算梯度以降低训练成本。合成数据（含噪声）实验表明该方法在宽范围变形速率与载荷路径下具有优异精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有黏弹性本构模型的标定通常需要难以在真实实验中直接获取的完整场内量（如内部应变/应力场），且需要满足热力学一致性与材料对称性等物理约束；同时直接通过所有牛顿迭代步反向传播求梯度的训练方式计算成本极高。因此需要一种仅依赖实验可直接测量的量（反作用力、表面位移）且训练高效的标定框架。

Method: 将PANN嵌入广义标准材料（GSM）理论，用自由能与对偶耗散势两个标量势函数构造本构模型，以不变式表示保证材料对称性。对薄试样采用平面应力假设，基于平衡间隙法构造约束优化问题，结合拟牛顿优化器与自动微分求解；未知离面应变由平面应力条件确定，内变量的演化采用隐式时间积分，在每个积分点与时间步上通过局部牛顿迭代求解非线性方程组。训练梯度采用后向伴随（adjoint）方法计算，而非对所有牛顿迭代步反向传播，从而大幅降低计算成本。

Result: 在合成数据（含噪声位移与力）上验证，该方法在宽范围的变形速率与载荷路径下均展现出与参考结果极好的一致性（excellent agreement），证明其标定精度与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该无监督、仅依赖可测量量的PANN标定框架能够以热力学一致且材料对称的方式高效、鲁棒地识别小应变黏弹性本构参数，兼具物理可解释性与较低的训练成本，为真实实验数据的应用提供了可行路径。

Abstract: We propose an unsupervised learning framework for calibrating a physics-augmented neural network (PANN) for small-strain viscoelasticity via full-field data. It only requires quantities that are directly accessible in real experiments for training, namely global reaction forces and surface displacements. The underlying PANN is embedded in the generalized standard materials theory, in which two scalar-valued potentials render the constitutive model thermodynamically consistent by construction, while invariant-based representations of the free energy and the dual dissipation potential additionally ensure material symmetry. Considering a thin specimen under the plane stress assumption, we formulate a constrained optimization problem based on the equilibrium gap method in combination with quasi-Newton optimizers and automatic differentiation. Thereby, the unknown out-of-plane strain follows from the plane stress condition and the evolution of the internal variables is captured by an implicit time integration scheme. The resulting system of nonlinear equations is solved via a local Newton iteration at quadrature point and time step. To drastically reduce the computational cost of training, the backward adjoint method is employed to compute the gradient of the target loss, instead of backpropagating through all Newton iteration steps. The proposed framework is demonstrated for synthetic data, including noisy displacements and forces, showing excellent agreement across a wide range of deformation rates and load paths.

</details>


### [268] [What Do CAE Simulation Agents Really Need Beyond a Generic Harness?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03718)
*Jiasheng Shi,Tianhan Zhang*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文研究现代LLM通用代理框架下CAE仿真代理还缺什么。在信息获取和修复预算固定的情况下，单代理框架（96.4%）超越多代理专用系统（88.2%）。消融实验表明收益来自执行反馈修复（71.8%→96.4%），脚本化反思无帮助，唯一仍有增益的输入是以求解器教程形式提供的领域知识（80.9%→96.4%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CAE仿真是工程中最庞大、要求最高的领域之一，设置OpenFOAM、FEniCS或COMSOL等求解器需要真正的专家知识。LLM代理有望将自然语言请求转化为可工作的仿真，近年CAE代理添加了多代理分解、领域检索和脚本化反思等仿真专用机制——但这些机制是为弱基础模型设计的。现代通用代理框架已具备多轮推理、工具使用和执行反馈，因此需要探究：在通用框架之上，CAE仿真代理究竟还需要什么。

Method: 在信息访问和修复预算固定的条件下，将单代理通用框架与多代理专用CAE系统进行对比实验；通过消融研究逐项测试执行反馈修复、脚本化反思和领域知识（求解器教程）对各组件贡献的影响，评估基准为FoamBench。

Result: 单代理通用框架在FoamBench上达到96.4%，超过多代理专用系统的88.2%。消融显示：执行反馈修复是关键能力，使其从71.8%提升至96.4%；脚本化反思没有额外贡献；提供求解器教程形式的领域知识是最大的单项增益来源（80.9%→96.4%）。

Conclusion: 现代通用代理框架已覆盖多代理机制的核心价值，CAE仿真代理无需再堆砌多代理分解或脚本化反思等专门组件；其真正瓶颈在于两件事：能够实际运行的执行反馈闭环，以及以求解器教程形式注入的领域知识。因此未来CAE代理的研究应聚焦于高质量、可检索的求解器知识供给，而非重复建设通用框架已有的推理与工具能力。

Abstract: Computer-aided engineering (CAE) simulation is among the largest and most demanding areas of engineering, where setting up a solver such as OpenFOAM, FEniCS, or COMSOL takes real expertise. Large language model (LLM) agents promise to turn a natural-language request into a working simulation, and recent CAE agents add simulation-specific machinery: multi-agent decomposition, domain retrieval, and scripted reflection. That machinery suited weak base models; modern harnesses already supply multi-turn reasoning, tool use, and execution feedback. We ask what a CAE simulation agent still needs beyond a generic harness. With information access and repair budget held fixed, a single-agent harness matches or beats multi-agent specialized systems (FoamBench 96.4\% vs.\ 88.2\%).
  Ablations trace this to capabilities the harness already provides: execution-feedback repair lifts FoamBench from 71.8\% with no repair round to 96.4\%, while scripted reflection adds nothing. The one input that still helps is domain knowledge supplied as solver tutorials, our largest measured gain (80.9\% to 96.4\%).

</details>


### [269] [sp-DBA: a general framework for adaptive transform-domain computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03922)
*Jingkun Jiang,Pingchuan Deng,Yang Xia*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文提出频谱动态块激活（sp-DBA），一种面向变换域工作流的自适应加速框架，通过动态激活计算块在不重建求解器的情况下实现最高28.1倍的变换域更新加速和8.4倍的整体加速，并在多达8个GPU的分布式FFT工作流中保持有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变换域方法简化了分析与计算，是科学计算和信号处理的核心，但现有的自适应策略往往引入新的数据结构或需要大量工作流重构，限制了在大规模并行硬件上的高效执行。因此需要一个无需重构现有求解器即可实现执行级自适应的加速框架。

Method: 提出谱动态块激活（sp-DBA）框架：在计算演化过程中动态激活变换域计算块，将计算集中在需要的区域，同时保持数值精度并保留优化的变换操作；通过在执行层面引入自适应性融入成熟的变换域工作流，无需重建现有求解器。

Result: 在材料科学、生物学和光通信等代表性工作流中，变换域更新最高加速28.1倍，整体加速最高8.4倍；在多达8个GPU上的强扩展和弱扩展测试表明，该局部自适应更新在分布式FFT工作流中依然有效。

Conclusion: sp-DBA通过向成熟的变换域工作流引入执行级自适应性且无需重建现有求解器，扩展了现代并行系统上科学模拟和信号处理计算的规模、时长与复杂度。

Abstract: Transform-domain methods simplify analysis and computation, making them central to scientific computing and signal processing. However, existing adaptive strategies often introduce new data structures or require substantial workflow redesign, limiting efficient execution on massively parallel hardware. Here we present spectral dynamic block activation (sp-DBA), an adaptive acceleration framework for transform-domain workflows. sp-DBA dynamically activates transform-domain computation blocks as a calculation evolves, concentrating computation where it is needed while retaining numerical accuracy and optimized transform operations. Across representative workflows in materials science, biology, and optical communications, the demonstrated implementations achieve speedups of up to 28.1-fold for transform-domain updates and up to 8.4-fold overall acceleration; strong- and weak-scaling tests on up to eight GPUs show that the local adaptive update remains effective within distributed FFT workflows. By introducing execution-level adaptivity into mature transform-domain workflows without rebuilding existing solvers, sp-DBA extends the scale, duration, and complexity of scientific simulations and signal-processing calculations on modern parallel systems.

</details>


### [270] [Differentiable Hybrid Modelling for Learning and Optimising Chemical Transport Processes from Experimental Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04011)
*Arthur Jessop,Mohammed Alsubeihi,Ben Moseley,Ashwin Kumar Rajagopalan*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通用的可微分混合建模框架，用于输运过程（特别是群体平衡方程）。该框架将 JAX 有限体积求解器与可学习的神经网络组件相结合，既能从真实实验数据中自动发现本构定律，又能拟合初始条件，并利用框架的可微分性实现过程优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统输运模型依赖人工挑选的本构定律，且常假设初始条件精确已知，这两点限制都会使模型预测产生显著偏差并在预测与控制场景中引入系统性误差；而黑箱神经替代模型虽能更好地匹配真实数据，却局限于训练任务且无法检验其物理一致性。

Method: 提出一个面向群体平衡方程的通用可微分混合建模框架：将 JAX 有限体积群体平衡求解器与可学习的神经网络组件集成，训练网络以同时发现本构定律并拟合初始条件，从而更准确地建模真实实验输运系统；同时利用框架的可微分性直接对实验设置进行优化以获得感兴趣的目标量。

Result: 该框架能够从真实实验数据中发现本构定律并拟合初始条件，从而更好地建模真实实验输运系统；借助可微分性，还可用于过程优化，直接优化实验设置以最大化/最小化目标量。工作展示了此类可微分混合建模框架在涉及质量、能量和/或动量输运的化学分离过程学习与优化中的巨大潜力。

Conclusion: 可微分混合建模框架通过融合物理求解器与可学习神经网络，克服了传统模型本构定律和初始条件假设带来的偏差，同时兼顾了数据拟合能力与物理一致性，可推广至任意涉及质量、能量和动量输运的化学分离过程的建模与优化。

Abstract: Reliable transport models are essential when modelling and optimising many chemical engineering processes, yet, most models assume hand-picked constitutive laws which may not reflect reality, and often assume initial conditions are known exactly. Both restrictions can significantly bias model predictions and lead to systematic error when used in predictive and control settings. Black-box neural surrogate alternatives for modelling can better match real example data, but are confined to the task they were trained on and cannot be interrogated for physical consistency. Here we introduce a general-purpose differentiable hybrid modelling framework for transport processes, specifically for the case of population balance equations. Our framework integrates a JAX finite volume population balance solver with learnable neural network components which are trained to both discover constitutive laws and fit initial conditions from real experimental data, allowing us to better model real experimental transport systems. Furthermore, we use our framework for process optimisation, using its differentiability to allow us to direct optimising experimental settings for quantities of interest. This work highlights the huge potential of such differentiable hybrid modelling frameworks for learning and optimising any given chemical separation which involves mass, energy, and/or momentum transport.

</details>


### [271] [Influence of Extruded Filament Shape on Buildability in 3D Concrete Printing: A Geometry-Informed Deep Learning-FEM Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04028)
*Giacomo Rizzieri,Saif-Ur-Rehman,Jörg F. Unger,Annika Robens-Radermacher*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种几何信息感知的建模框架，将基于深度学习的丝材形状预测工具ShapeGen3DCP与层激活有限元法（FEM）相结合，用于评估3D混凝土打印结构的可建造性。该框架可直接从材料和工艺参数生成考虑真实几何形状的数值模型，无需实验表征或流体模拟。研究表明，挤出参数和丝材几何形状显著影响可建造性预测，椭圆近似在精度与建模复杂度之间达到最佳平衡；若采用矩形表示，基于体积守恒确定尺寸比基于最大丝宽或层间接触宽度校准更可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在3D混凝土打印（3DCP）可建造性评估中，沉积丝材的几何形态会显著影响结构性能和稳定性，但大多数基于有限元（FEM）的方法将打印层简化为矩形，限制了预测精度，亟需考虑真实丝材几何形状的建模方法。

Method: 提出一个几何信息感知建模框架，集成基于深度学习的丝材形状预测工具ShapeGen3DCP与层激活FEM方法；直接从材料和工艺参数生成几何感知的数值模型，无需实验表征或计算密集的流体流动模拟；并通过实验数据验证及直线墙体参数化研究进行评估。

Result: 实验验证和参数化研究表明，挤出参数及由此产生的丝材几何形状对可建造性预测有显著影响；真实丝材表示对自由流动沉积尤为重要，而层压策略对几何简化不太敏感；在所研究的表示中，椭圆近似在几何保真度和建模简单性之间取得最佳平衡；若需使用矩形表示以简化网格并加快计算，基于体积守恒确定其尺寸比用最大丝宽或层间接触宽度校准更可靠。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法证明了将丝材几何形状纳入3DCP模拟的重要性，并为选择高效、准确的可建造性评估几何表示提供了实用指导。

Abstract: The geometric morphology of deposited filaments can significantly influence the structural performance and stability of 3D concrete-printed (3DCP) structures. However, most finite element (FEM)-based approaches for buildability assessment represent printed layers as simplified rectangles, potentially limiting predictive accuracy. This study proposes a geometry-informed modelling framework that integrates the deep-learning-based filament shape prediction tool ShapeGen3DCP with a layer-activation FEM approach to investigate the effect of realistic filament geometries on buildability. The framework generates geometry-aware numerical models directly from material and process parameters, eliminating the need for experimental filament characterization or computationally intensive fluid-flow simulations. Validation against experimental data and a parametric study of rectilinear walls demonstrate that extrusion parameters and the resulting filament geometry can significantly influence buildability predictions. Realistic filament representations are particularly important for free-flow deposition, whereas layer-pressing strategies are less sensitive to geometric simplifications. Among the investigated representations, an elliptical approximation provides an effective balance between geometric fidelity and modelling simplicity. When rectangular representations are preferred to enable regular computational meshes for faster simulations, defining their dimensions based on volume conservation improves prediction reliability compared with calibrating them using either the maximum filament width or the interlayer contact width. Overall, the proposed methodology demonstrates the importance of incorporating filament geometry into 3DCP simulations and provides practical guidance for selecting efficient and accurate geometric representations for buildability assessment.

</details>


<div id='cs.IT'></div>

# cs.IT [[Back]](#toc)

### [272] [Direct Satellite-to-Device Communications: From Cooperative Task Offloading to Non-Cooperative Access Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02955)
*Sai Huang,Wanli Ni,Ke Lv,Pengcheng Zhang,Yurui Zheng,Menghan Zhang,Zihui Gong,Zhiyong Feng*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文面向6G非地面网络中的直连卫星到设备（DS2D）通信，提出一种多功能DS2D系统，兼顾协作式任务卸载与非协作式接入监测。在协作场景中，结合信道估计模块与决斗双深度Q网络（D3QN）动态优化任务卸载策略；在非协作场景中，提出基于Transformer的模型实现盲信号检测与自动调制分类（AMC）。仿真表明D3QN相比静态关联策略可将平均时延降低最多225%，信号检测平均存在概率达90.5%，AMC在低信噪比区间相比现有方法有9.4%的精度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DS2D通信有望将泛在连接与边缘计算能力扩展到6G非地面网络的偏远及服务不足地区，但实际部署面临双重关键挑战：其一是协作场景中动态卫星信道条件（如严重多普勒频移、快衰落）以及受限的卫星计算资源；其二是非协作场景中未经授权的卫星接入会带来显著的频谱安全威胁。现有方法难以同时应对这两类挑战。

Method: 提出一个通用的DS2D系统，支持协作任务卸载与非协作接入监测两套体系。在协作DS2D通信中，将信道估计模块与决斗双深度Q网络（D3QN）集成，利用信道状态信息动态优化任务卸载策略，以应对动态信道与计算资源受限问题。在非协作DS2D通信中，提出基于Transformer的模型，分别用于盲信号检测（判断DS2D信号是否存在）和自动调制分类（AMC），以应对未经授权接入带来的频谱安全威胁。

Result: 仿真结果有三点：1) D3QN算法相比静态关联策略可将平均时延降低最多225%；2) 信号检测模型对DS2D信号的平均存在检测概率达到90.5%；3) 提出的AMC算法在不同信噪比（SNR）下均表现出优越性能，在低信噪比区间相比现有方法有9.4%的精度提升。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，通过将强化学习（D3QN）用于协作式任务卸载、将Transformer架构用于非协作式频谱感知与信号识别，可以有效兼顾DS2D系统的时延性能与频谱安全，为6G非地面网络中DS2D通信的实用化部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Direct satellite-to-device (DS2D) communication is emerging as a transformative paradigm for extending ubiquitous connectivity and edge computing capabilities to remote and underserved regions within 6G non-terrestrial networks. However, practical deployment faces dual critical challenges: i) dynamic satellite channel conditions (e.g., severe Doppler shifts, fast fading) and constrained satellite computing resources in cooperative scenarios; and ii) unauthorized satellite access introduces significant spectrum security threats in non-cooperative scenarios. To address these challenges, we propose a versatile DS2D system that supports cooperative task offloading and non-cooperative access monitoring. For cooperative DS2D communications, we integrate a channel estimation module with a dueling double deep Q-network (D3QN) to dynamically optimize task offloading strategy. For non-cooperative DS2D communications, we propose Transformer-based models to enable blind signal detection and automatic modulation classification (AMC). Simulation results show that: 1) The D3QN algorithm reduces average latency by up to 225\% compared to static association policies. 2) Our signal detection model achieves an average presence detection probability of 90.5\% for DS2D signals. 3) The proposed AMC algorithm achieves superior performance across different signal-to-noise ratios (SNRs), with a 9.4\% accuracy gain in low-SNR regimes compared to existing methods.

</details>


### [273] [Adaptive Beam Hopping and Power Control for Dual-Layer Over-the-Air Online Federated Learning in LEO Satellite Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03202)
*Zhendong Li,Shaojie Wang,Zhou Su,Zihao Zhang,Haixia Peng,Nan Cheng,Ying Wang,Wen Chen*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究低轨（LEO）卫星网络中基于空中计算（OTA）的在线联邦学习（FL），构建地面设备—卫星—数据处理中心的双层OTA聚合架构，并将长期数据利用率最大化问题建模为受波束预算、发射功率和全局均方误差（MSE）约束的混合整数非线性规划（MINLP）。由于问题NP难且具有时变拓扑与动态数据特性，作者将其建模为马尔可夫决策过程，提出基于近端策略优化（PPO）的深度强化学习框架联合优化自适应波束跳变与功率控制，并采用MSE感知奖励平衡数据利用率与聚合精度。仿真表明该算法在长期数据利用率与FL收敛速度上优于基准方案，同时满足MSE约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LEO卫星网络中的在线联邦学习面临设备持续采集新数据而样本新鲜度随训练时间衰减的挑战；同时，OTA聚合尽管通信高效，却引入了端到端聚合失真。现有工作缺乏对双层OTA聚合架构下波束跳变（离散）与功率控制（连续）联合调度及数据新鲜度约束的考虑，而该耦合的MINLP问题NP难解且环境时变，亟需自适应的在线调度方案。

Method: 提出一种采用近端策略优化（PPO）的深度强化学习框架：将问题建模为马尔可夫决策过程，状态包含时变卫星拓扑与动态数据信息，动作空间涵盖离散的波束跳变决策和连续的功率控制；通过MSE感知的奖励函数在最大化数据利用率的同时约束端到端聚合精度，从而联合优化两层OTA聚合中的自适应波束跳变与功率分配。

Result: 数值仿真结果表明，所提PPO-based算法在所有基准方案中表现最优，获得更高的长期数据利用率和更快的FL收敛速度，同时始终满足全局MSE约束要求。

Conclusion: 本文验证了深度强化学习（PPO）能够有效解决LEO卫星网络中双层OTA联邦学习这一NP难的、具有离散步长与连续控制耦合的在线调度问题，在保持聚合精度（MSE约束）的前提下显著提升长期数据利用率与FL收敛性能，为卫星网络中的高效在线学习提供了可行方案。

Abstract: This paper investigates over-the-air (OTA) computation enabled online federated learning (FL) in low-Earth orbit (LEO) satellite networks. Specifically, we consider a dual-layer OTA aggregation architecture, where ground devices upload analog model updates to serving satellites via uplink OTA aggregation, and satellites forward the aggregated signals to a data processing center through the second round OTA aggregation. Then, we formulate a long-term data-utilization maximization problem in which devices continuously collect new data and untrained samples gradually lose freshness. The problem is subject to the satellite beam budget, transmit-power limit, and global mean squared error (MSE) constraint that governs end-to-end aggregation distortion. This yields a coupled mixed-integer nonlinear programming (MINLP) problem, involving tightly coupled discrete beam-hopping decisions and continuous power control. Due to the combinatorial action space and nonconvex constraints, the problem is NP-hard and computationally intractable. Furthermore, the time-varying satellite topology and dynamic data generation render it a sequential decision-making problem, necessitating adaptive online scheduling. To address these issues, we cast the problem as a Markov decision process and develop a proximal policy optimization (PPO)-based deep reinforcement learning framework that jointly optimizes adaptive beam hopping and power control, using an MSE-aware reward to balance data utilization and aggregation accuracy. Numerical simulation results verify that the proposed algorithm consistently outperforms other benchmark schemes, achieving superior long-term data utilization and faster FL convergence while satisfying the MSE requirement.

</details>


### [274] [Variational Probabilistic Quantization for Secret Key Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03205)
*Xinyang Li,Vlad C. Andrei,Peter J. Gu,Yiqi Chen,Ullrich J. Mönich,Holger Boche*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 提出变分概率量化（VPQ）框架，用神经网络编码器将相关源直接映射为离散密钥字母表，并以与单向密钥率三项（熵、失配、泄漏）对应的变分对抗目标训练；配合线性码偏移安全草图完成密钥一致化，无需单独的隐私放大步骤，理论上证明了其损失是单向密钥容量的下界，并在高斯衰落信道上优于经典与近期学习基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于相关观测的物理层密钥生成中，经典流程存在三点不足：1）量化靠人工设计；2）隐私放大作为独立后处理步骤；3）整个流程未针对密钥率这一核心目标进行端到端优化。这导致系统难以逼近密钥生成的理论容量。

Method: 提出变分概率量化（VPQ）：1）使用神经编码器将相关源观测直接映射为离散密钥字母表；2）采用变分对抗训练目标，其熵项、失配项与泄漏项分别对应单向密钥率的三个组成部分；3）以线性码偏移安全草图对编码器输出进行调和，得到一致密钥而不需要单独的隐私放大步骤；4）理论上证明VPQ损失为所诱导源的单向密钥容量下界，并针对给定字母表大小与失配概率的源类别，闭式推导出由有限域线性草图达到的最坏情形最优密钥率。

Result: 在高斯衰落信道实验中，VPQ相比一个经典基线和两个近期基于学习的方法，向相关窃听者泄漏的信息更少；且Reed–Solomon调和方案的性能落在预测的有限块长差距之内，验证了理论分析的准确性。

Conclusion: VPQ将量化、一致化（调和）与隐私保护统一到一个端到端可微、且以密钥率为目标的框架中，兼具理论保证（容量的可证明下界与闭式最坏情形最优率）和实际优势（更低泄漏、有限块长预测准确），为物理层安全密钥生成提供了新的学习式范式。

Abstract: Secret key generation from correlated observations at Alice and Bob, in the presence of an eavesdropper Eve, underpins physical-layer security. Classical pipelines quantize by hand, amplify privacy afterwards, and optimize no objective tied to a key rate. We propose Variational Probabilistic Quantization (VPQ): neural encoders that map the correlated sources directly into a discrete key alphabet, trained by a variational adversarial objective whose entropy, mismatch, and leakage terms match the three terms of the one-way secret key rate. A linear code-offset secure sketch then reconciles the encoder outputs into an identical key without a separate privacy amplification step. We prove that the VPQ losses lower-bound the one-way secret key capacity of the induced source, and derive in closed form the optimal worst-case key rate over the source class of a given alphabet size and mismatch probability, attained by finite-field linear sketches. On Gaussian fading channels, VPQ leaks less to a correlated eavesdropper than one classical and two recent learning-based baselines, and Reed--Solomon reconciliation operates within the predicted finite-blocklength gap.

</details>


### [275] [A Joint Power-Privacy Control Framework for Decentralized Learning over Heterogeneous Wireless Multicasting Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03245)
*Amir Ziaeddini,Yauhen Yakimenka,Joerg Kliewer*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 提出一种面向无线多播网络的去中心化学习框架，将功率控制与差分隐私保护相结合，通过功率分配策略在模型系数与注入高斯噪声之间分配发射功率，在每轮迭代实现规定的最大隐私泄漏约束，并证明累计遗憾界为 O(log T)，在 CIFAR-10 上的 IID 与非 IID 场景中表现出优于现有方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在无线多播网络中，去中心化学习面临两大挑战：一是无线信道的非对称信道增益与各节点异构的发射功率上限尚未被充分建模；二是在资源受限的无线节点上，隐私保护（差分隐私）与学习性能之间存在功率竞争，需要显式的联合控制机制来同时保证隐私与收敛性能。

Method: 构建基于行随机邻接矩阵的通信网络模型以刻画非对称信道增益和异构最大发射功率；提出显式功率分配策略，将每个节点的有限最大发射功率在模型系数传输与注入高斯噪声之间进行分配，从而同时控制学习性能与隐私泄漏；基于该框架设计去中心化学习算法并对累计遗憾提供理论分析。

Result: 理论上证明所提算法达到 O(log T) 的累计遗憾界；在 CIFAR-10 数据集上的 IID 与非 IID 分布、多种隐私级别、不同客户端数量及多种图拓扑下开展综合实验，结果显示算法在各类设置下均表现良好，且优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的去中心化学习框架能够在真实的无线通信约束下，通过功率分配策略同时实现差分隐私保证与高效联合训练，在隐私保护与学习性能之间取得良好平衡，具有实际部署价值。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a decentralized learning framework that incorporates both power control and privacy guarantees. Specifically, we enable a set of clients in a wireless multicast network to jointly train a common model while maintaining a prescribed per-iteration maximum privacy leakage level. The communication network is represented by a rowstochastic adjacency matrix, allowing us to capture asymmetric channel gains as well as heterogeneous maximum transmit power levels. Differential privacy is enforced through an explicit powersplitting strategy that allocates each node's limited maximum transmit power between model coefficients and injected Gaussian noise, thereby jointly controlling learning performance and privacy leakage. We further prove that the proposed algorithm achieves a cumulative regret bound of O(logT), whereTdenotes the time horizon. To evaluate the practical performance of our approach, we perform comprehensive experiments on the CIFAR-10 dataset under both IID and non-IID data distributions, considering different privacy levels, diverse numbers of clients, and various graph topologies. The results demonstrate strong performance across the considered settings and improved performance over existing methods, highlighting the effectiveness of the proposed algorithm under realistic wireless communication constraints.

</details>


### [276] [Iterative Semantic Decoding for Short Block Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03256)
*Jiafu Hao,Chentao Yue,Mingcheng Nie,Branka Vucetic,Yonghui Li*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于迭代增强的语义接收机，用于在噪声无线信道上使用多个短分组码传输自然语言文本。发送端对每个句子进行字符级交织、分段并独立编码；接收端通过信道译码器与语言模型之间的迭代译码，利用解交织分散突发错误，并将经信道观测校验的降噪字符作为语义信息反馈给信道译码器。在SNLI语料库和AWGN信道上的仿真表明，与传统短分组编码相比，所提接收机可获得约1.5 dB的BLER增益，且在信噪比高于1.0 dB时BLEU与ROUGE分数保持在99%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统短分组码针对自然语言文本传输时未充分利用文本的语义结构，且短分组码译码后易产生突发错误，导致端到端文本质量（如BLEU/ROUGE）下降。现有方案缺乏一种能够将信道观测信息与语言先验知识有效融合、并跨码块协同纠错的迭代接收机制。

Method: 发送端：每个句子经字符级交织器置换后划分为若干段，各段由短分组码独立编码。接收端采用迭代译码器，包含信道译码器和语言模型两部分，二者之间通过解交织器连接以将各码块内的突发译码错误分散到整个句子。每次迭代中，语言模型对信道译码输出进行降噪，并将经信道观测一致性校验的降噪字符作为语义信息反馈给信道译码器，用于下一轮迭代译码。

Result: 在斯坦福自然语言推理（SNLI）语料库和加性高斯白噪声（AWGN）信道上的仿真表明，与传统短分组编码相比，所提接收机可实现约1.5 dB的BLER增益；当信噪比高于1.0 dB时，BLEU和ROUGE评分均保持在99%以上。

Conclusion: 该迭代语义接收机通过信道译码与语言建模的交替迭代、以及经信道校验的语义信息反馈，有效利用了自然语言的语义冗余来修正突发错误，在提升BLER性能的同时保持了极高的文本质量，验证了语义信息与信道编码联合迭代处理的可行性。

Abstract: This paper proposes an iteratively enhanced semantic receiver for natural-language text transmission over noisy wireless channels using multiple short block codes. At the transmitter, each sentence is permuted by a character-level interleaver, partitioned into segments, and independently encoded by short block codes. At the receiver, we develop an iterative decoder consisting of a channel decoder and a language model, where a de-interleaver between them disperses the burst decoding errors within each segment across the sentence. In each iteration, the language model denoises the channel decoding output, and the denoised characters verified to be consistent with the channel observations are fed back to the channel decoder as semantic information for the next iteration. Simulation results on the Stanford Natural Language Inference (SNLI) corpus over the additive white Gaussian noise (AWGN) channel show that the proposed receiver achieves approximately 1.5 dB block error rate (BLER) gain over conventional short-block coding, while maintaining BLEU and ROUGE scores above 99% at SNRs beyond 1.0 dB.

</details>


### [277] [Finite-SNR Closed Form for the Square Marčenko-Pastur MIMO Capacity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03356)
*Mohamed Akrout,Robert W. Heath*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 针对IID单用户MIMO信道中发射/接收天线数之比为平方（1:1）这一特殊情形的容量计算缺口，本文提出经典三角参数化方法，用一个单一角度替代移动水线截止点，从而将SNR与容量均表示为该角度的显式函数，最终以扫掠角度而非逐点求解标量约束的方式绘制容量曲线，并以闭合形式给出低SNR行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在满CSIT的IID单用户MIMO信道中，每接收天线的渐近容量是对Marčenko-Pastur定律的水注（waterfilling）积分，其形状仅取决于发射/接收天线数之比。除平方比之外的所有比值都有闭合形式的容量表达式，唯独平方比情形因Marčenko-Pastur支撑集延伸到原点而行为不同：在任意有限SNR下水注截止点严格保持在支撑集内部，导致水水位（water level）此前只能通过数值方法求得，缺乏闭合形式解。

Method: 采用经典三角参数化方法处理平方比下的Marčenko-Pastur定律。该参数化消除了边界奇异性，将移动的截止点替换为单一角度参数；由此SNR与容量均成为该角度的显式函数，容量曲线通过扫掠角度而非在每个工作点求解标量约束来获得。同一参数化还可直接导出低SNR行为（容量对SNR的斜率等于极限谱的上边缘）的闭合形式。

Result: 平方比情形下SNR与容量均被表示为单一角度的显式函数，容量曲线可通过角度扫掠完整绘制；低SNR下容量关于SNR的斜率在闭合形式下等于极限谱上边缘，从而补齐了该情形下此前仅有数值解的空缺。

Conclusion: 该工作以经典三角参数化填补了平方比MIMO信道容量闭合形式解的空白，使该特殊情形与其他比值一样享有解析表达，并且一次性给出了低SNR斜率的闭合形式，为该问题的数值瓶颈提供了简洁的解析替代方案。

Abstract: For the canonical independent and identically distributed (IID) single-user multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) channel with full channel state information at the transmitter (CSIT), the asymptotic capacity per receive antenna is a waterfilling integral over the Marčenko-Pastur law, whose shape depends only on the ratio of transmit to receive antennas. Closed forms are known for every ratio other than the square one, and there only above a finite signal-to-noise ratio (SNR). The square ratio behaves differently because the Marčenko-Pastur support reaches the origin. The waterfilling cutoff then stays strictly inside the support at every finite SNR, and the water level has been available only numerically. This letter closes that gap with a classical trigonometric parametrization of the square law, which removes the edge singularity and replaces the moving cutoff by a single angle. Both the SNR and the capacity become explicit functions of that angle, so the capacity curve is traced by sweeping the angle rather than by solving a scalar constraint at each operating point. The same parametrization delivers the low-SNR behavior in closed form, where the slope of capacity in SNR equals the upper edge of the limiting spectrum.

</details>


### [278] [TokenComSR: Task-Sensitivity-Guided Token Communication for Wireless Image Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03735)
*Ye Wang,Li Qiao,Zhen Gao,Hua Wang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: TokenComSR 提出了一种面向资源受限无线图像传输的令牌通信框架，通过任务敏感功率分配（TSPA）与信噪比条件令牌细化模块（TRM），结合 Swin Transformer 令牌收发器和接收端超分辨率模块，在带宽受限衰落信道上超越了分离编码和 JSCC-SR 基线，兼顾重建保真度与感知质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无线边缘设备在带宽受限衰落信道上采用传统分离编码会遭受悬崖效应（cliff-effect）导致的性能崩溃；而基于 CNN 的深度联合信源信道编码（JSCC）虽能缓解该问题，却难以保持补丁级（patch-level）结构，从而无法实现自适应的逐令牌功率分配，也限制了令牌域对超分辨率（SR）的补偿。

Method: 提出令牌通信与超分辨率联合框架 TokenComSR，包含两个关键模块：(1) 任务敏感功率分配（TSPA），将训练阶段估计的任务敏感度蒸馏为推理时的令牌功率权重；(2) 信噪比条件令牌细化模块（TRM），估计 SNR 条件下的残差以在解码前纠正信道引起的令牌域失真。框架将 Swin Transformer 令牌收发器与接收端 SR 模块配对用于资源受限的无线图像传输。

Result: 仿真结果验证了 TSPA 与 TRM 的有效性，在重建保真度和感知质量两个指标上均优于分离编码及 JSCC-SR 基线方法。

Conclusion: TokenComSR 通过令牌域的功率分配与 SNR 条件化纠错，有效克服了传统分离编码的悬崖效应与现有 JSCC 在补丁级结构保持上的不足，为资源受限无线图像传输提供了更优的保真度与感知质量折中方案。

Abstract: For resource-constrained wireless edge devices over bandwidth-limited fading channels, wireless image transmission using traditional separate coding suffers from the cliff-effect collapse. Prevailing deep joint source-channel coding (JSCC) based on convolutional neural networks can mitigate this issue but usually fail to preserve patch-level structures, thereby preventing adaptive per-token power allocation and limiting token-domain compensation for super-resolution (SR). To address these challenges, we propose a token communication framework with SR (TokenComSR). Specifically, we conceive a task-sensitive power allocation (TSPA) module and a signal-to-noise ratio (SNR)-conditioned token refinement module (TRM). TSPA distills training estimates of task sensitivity into inference token power weights, while TRM estimates an SNR-conditioned residual to correct channel-induced distortion in the token domain before decoding. Building on TSPA and TRM, the proposed TokenComSR pairs a Swin Transformer-based token transceiver with a receiver-side SR module for resource-constrained wireless image transmission. Simulation results confirm the effectiveness of the proposed TSPA and TRM, demonstrating improvements over separate coding and JSCC-SR baselines in both reconstruction fidelity and perceptual quality.

</details>


### [279] [A Quantization Problem Posed by Adaptive Streaming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03745)
*Yuriy A. Reznik*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文将自适应比特率（ABR）流媒体中编码阶梯（encoding ladder）的选择形式化为一个带宽分布上的标量量化问题。该量化具有特殊性质：客户端逻辑将每个量化单元的再生值固定在该单元的左边缘，且失真度量是单侧质量损失而非均方误差。研究发现，最优 n 档阶梯的质量差距 G_n^* 以 Θ(1/n) 衰减（而非教科书式的 Θ(1/n^2)），并给出了渐近常数 C_w 及最优速率密度 √(p·Q')（替代 Panter–Dite 的 p^{1/3}）。由此推导出分位数档位放置、达到容差所需的档位数 n(ε)≈C_w/ε 以及经济最优阶梯规模 n^*=√(λC_w/κ) 等闭式设计规则，并可推广到分辨率、编解码器、感知质量指标及二维自适应逻辑等场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 ABR 流媒体中，每个标题被编码为多个比特率档位（即编码阶梯），客户端选择带宽所能支撑的最高档位。阶梯（档位集合）的选择会同时影响各档的交付质量、播放适配效果与运营成本。但现有理论缺乏对该选择问题的严格刻画——作者发现阶梯选择本质上是带宽分布上的标量量化，但其约束与经典失真（平方误差）量化不同，因而需要重塑经典量化理论以给出最优设计与性能极限。

Method: 作者将编码阶梯刻画为带宽分布上的标量量化问题，其核心特征为：客户端逻辑将每个量化单元（比特率区间）的再生值（所交付档位）钉在该单元的左边缘（最低保障速率处），失真度量采用单侧质量损失（低于最优质量即损失）而非平方误差。在该特殊约束下，作者推导出最优 n 档阶梯的质量差距 G_n^* 的衰减阶为 Θ(1/n)，并给出其渐近极限 n·G_n^*→C_w=½(∫√(p·Q')dB)²，其中最优速率档位分布遵循密度 ∝√(p·Q')（替代经典 Panter–Dite 的 p^{1/3}）。基于该定律，进一步导出分位数档位放置的闭式规则、容差所需档位数 n(ε)≈C_w/ε，以及考虑运营成本 κ 与质量差距定价 λ 的经济最优阶梯规模 n^*=√(λC_w/κ)。

Result: 与经典均方误差量化的 Θ(1/n²) 不同，最优 n 档编码阶梯的质量差距以 Θ(1/n) 衰减；渐近极限由 C_w=½(∫√(p·Q')dB)² 给出，且最优速率密度为 √(p·Q')。该定律直接产生闭式设计规则：分位数档位放置、档位数 n(ε)≈C_w/ε、经济最优规模 n^*=√(λC_w/κ)。分析同样适用于不同视频分辨率、编解码器、感知质量指标以及现代 Web 播放器的二维（如带宽-分辨率）自适应逻辑下的阶梯设计。

Conclusion: 该研究为 ABR 编码阶梯设计提供了严格且可操作的量化理论基础：质量差距的 Θ(1/n) 衰减律揭示了在资源受限下增加档位的收益递减规律，而闭式公式可直接指导实际阶梯的档位选择与规模决策。方法的一般性使其可扩展至分辨率、编解码器、质量指标与多维自适应等更丰富的实际场景，具有理论与实践双重价值。

Abstract: In adaptive bitrate (ABR) streaming, the delivery technology behind most Internet video, each title is encoded at several bitrates, forming an \emph{encoding ladder} of \emph{renditions}. Each client plays the highest rendition that its network bandwidth can sustain. We show that choosing the ladder is a problem of \emph{scalar quantization} of the bandwidth distribution. However, this quantization problem is of an unusual kind: the client's logic pins each quantization cell's reproduction value to the cell's \emph{left edge}, and the distortion measure is a one-sided quality loss rather than a squared error. The constraint reshapes the classical theory. The \emph{quality gap} of a ladder is the distance between its average delivered quality and the \emph{quality limit} that infinitely many renditions would attain. For optimal $n$-rendition ladders the gap $\mathcal{G}_n^*$ decays as $Θ(1/n)$, not the textbook $Θ(1/n^2)$. Moreover, with large $n$, $n\,\mathcal{G}_n^*\to C_w=\frac{1}{2}(\int\!\sqrt{p\,Q'}\,\,d B)^2$, with the limit attained when the ladder's rates follow the density $\sqrt{p\,Q'}$, which replaces Panter--Dite's $p^{1/3}$; here $p$ is the bandwidth density and $Q$ the quality--rate curve of the content. The law yields closed-form design rules: quantile rung placement, the rendition count $n(\varepsilon)\approx C_w/\varepsilon$ needed to reach tolerance $\varepsilon$, and the economic ladder size $n^*=\sqrt{λC_w/κ}$ when a rendition costs $κ$ to operate and the quality gap is priced at $λ$. The same analysis extends to the design of ladders employing different video resolutions, codecs, perceptual quality metrics, and the two-dimensional adaptation logic of modern web players.

</details>


### [280] [Hierarchical Beam Training and Codebook Design for Movable Antenna-Assisted Near-Field Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03776)
*Meihui Liu,Qian Zhang,Xuejun Cheng,Yuhui Jiao,Yunxiao Li,Ju Liu*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 针对6G近场通信中波束训练开销过大的问题，提出基于可移动天线（MA）的分层波束训练策略及对应分层码本，在联合角度-距离域形成聚焦波束，以低开销实现高波束增益，仿真验证其性能优于固定位置天线系统与远场波束训练方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 6G通信向更高频段和更大阵列孔径演进，近场范围迅速扩展，近场信道估计日益重要。由于球面波传播特性，近场波束训练需要在角度和距离域进行联合搜索，导致训练开销难以承受。可移动天线（MA）技术通过灵活重构天线位置，能够充分利用信道空间变化并实现更精确的波束聚焦，为高效波束训练设计提供了额外自由度。

Method: 基于MA辅助的近场系统，设计了一种分层波束训练策略，在降低训练开销的同时保持高波束增益，并设计了相应的分层码本。该码本在联合角度-距离域形成聚焦波束，在目标区域内最大化波束增益，同时抑制向非目标区域的能量泄漏。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提方法相比传统固定位置天线（FPA）系统及远场波束训练方法，可获得显著的性能增益。

Conclusion: MA辅助的近场分层波束训练能够有效缓解近场波束训练开销过高的问题，实现训练开销与波束增益之间的良好权衡，为6G近场通信中的高效CSI获取提供了可行方案。

Abstract: As sixth-generation (6G) communication systems evolve toward higher frequency bands and larger array apertures, the near-field range expands rapidly, making near-field channel estimation increasingly important and challenging. Beam training has been recognized as an effective approach for channel state information (CSI) acquisition. However, because of the propagation characteristics of spherical waves, beam training needs to perform a joint search in the angle and distance domains, which results in unaffordable beam training overhead. By flexibly reconfiguring antenna positions, movable antenna (MA) technology can fully exploit the spatial variations of wireless channels and achieve more accurate beam focusing, thereby providing additional flexibility for efficient beam training design. Therefore, based on MA-assisted near-field systems, we develop a hierarchical beam training strategy that combines reduced training overhead with high beam gain and design a corresponding hierarchical codebook. This codebook forms focused beams over the joint angle-distance domain, maximizing beam gain within the target region while suppressing energy leakage into non-target regions. Simulation results confirm substantial performance gains of the method over the conventional fixed-position antenna (FPA) system and the far-field beam training method.

</details>


### [281] [On the Impact of Site-Specific Training for a Real-World 5G NR System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04004)
*Reinhard Wiesmayr,Nuri Berke Baytekin,Chris Dick,Christoph Studer*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文基于苏黎世联邦理工学院的5G NR标准测试平台，通过真实测量数据系统研究了三类接收机架构（全可训练神经网络接收机、模型驱动的神经网络接收机和基于模型的接收机）的站点特定微调，涵盖了相隔六个月以上的双测量活动以及双流上行传输场景，并评估了基于站点特定协方差矩阵的LMMSE信道估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 站点特定训练能在不增加计算复杂度的前提下提升无线接收机性能，但已有真实场景研究仅局限于全可训练的神经接收机和单层（单流）传输，缺乏对多种接收机架构在真实标准合规测试平台上的多流传输与长期稳定性验证。

Method: 使用标准合规的5G NR测试平台（ETH Zurich）采集新测量数据，包括双流上行链路以及相隔六个月以上的多次测量活动；对全可训练神经网络、模型驱动神经网络和基于模型三类接收机进行站点特定微调；并利用合成信道或站点特定测量估计的协方差矩阵进行站点特定LMMSE信道估计，与迭代检测译码相结合。

Result: 站点特定微调大幅提升全可训练和模型驱动神经接收机性能，而对可调参数较少的基于模型接收机仅带来微小增益；单个联合微调的双流/单流神经接收机性能接近分别微调的接收机；微调效果在相隔六个月以上的测量活动中依然有效；站点特定LMMSE信道估计结合迭代检测译码在所测数据上取得了最低误码率。

Conclusion: 站点特定训练是提升无线接收机实际性能的有效且泛化稳健的手段，尤其对可训练参数多的神经接收机收益显著；站点特定的信道统计信息能够进一步优化基于模型的接收机，达到数据集中最佳性能。代码与数据集已公开以促进可复现研究。

Abstract: Site-specific training can improve wireless receiver performance without increasing computational complexity. However, real-world results have so far focused on fully trainable neural receivers and single-layer transmissions. We study site-specific finetuning of three receiver architectures: fully trainable neural, model-driven neural, and model-based. We train and evaluate these receivers using new measurements from a standard-compliant 5G NR testbed at ETH Zurich with dual-layer uplink transmission, including measurement campaigns conducted more than six months apart. Our results show that site-specific finetuning (i) substantially improves fully trainable and model-driven neural receivers, while resulting in only marginal gains for the less tunable model-based receiver; (ii) enables a single neural receiver jointly finetuned for single- and dual-layer transmission to closely match receivers finetuned separately for each configuration; and (iii) remains effective across measurement campaigns separated by more than six months. We also investigate site-specific linear minimum mean-square error channel estimation using covariance matrices estimated from either synthetic channels or site-specific measurements. When combined with iterative detection and decoding, site-specific channel estimation achieves the lowest error rate observed in our datasets. Our finetuning code and measurement datasets are publicly available at https://github.com/IIP-Group/site_specific_training

</details>


### [282] [Synchronization Strings over the Optimal Alphabet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04122)
*Huibo Xu,Shi Fu,Youming Qiao,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文解决了同步字符串的最小字母表大小问题：证明三字母表（ternary）即可构造任意长度的 2001/2002-同步字符串，从而确定三个符号是常数字母表大小的精确最小值。核心工具是一个“局部熵传递定理”，配以 Brinkhuis 族中的逐出现分支、删除球估计与不对称 Lovász 局部引理，同时导出指数多个有效词、Las Vegas 多项式时间构造、同步圆及一类极值无平方词的同步结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同步字符串能为数据流附加确定性位置标签以抵御插入与删除操作。已知四符号足以支持某个固定参数 epsilon<1，而两符号无法支持任意长的同步字符串，因此三符号是否可行成为遗留的关键缺口。确定“三是精确的最小常数字母表大小”不仅补全了这一问题的答案，也标志着该最小性问题的彻底解决。

Method: 作者提出局部熵传递定理：每个具有正条件最小熵的无平方块局部源都能以固定间隔支撑同步字符串。该定理通过 Brinkhuis 族中的逐出现分支实例化，保证每个长区间在暴露其外所有选择后仍保留线性条件最小熵。随后用删除球估计把该熵转化为高最长公共子序列的反集中性质，并用不对称 Lovász 局部引理同时施加所有区间约束；另有基于 54-均匀族的计算机辅助细化以逼近任意 epsilon>226/227 的情形。

Result: 每个长度都允许三元 2001/2002-同步字符串，从而证明三符号是常数字母表大小的精确最小值；基于更大 54-均匀族的计算机辅助改进进一步给出对任意 epsilon>226/227 的三元 epsilon-同步字符串。该框架还额外得到指数多个有效词、Las Vegas 多项式时间构造、同步圆以及一类极值无平方词内的同步。

Conclusion: 三元情况既是最小可行情形又具有丰富的构造性成果：三符号不仅是精确的最小常数字母表大小，还能在接近最优参数下通过高效（多项式时间随机化）方式构造，并推广到圆与极值无平方词等变体。

Abstract: Synchronization strings attach deterministic position labels to a stream that survive insertions and deletions. Haeupler and Shahrasbi introduced these objects, and subsequent work proved that four symbols suffice for some fixed parameter epsilon < 1, whereas two symbols cannot support arbitrarily long synchronization strings. We resolve the remaining ternary case: every length admits a ternary 2001/2002-synchronization string. Thus three is the exact minimum constant alphabet size. A computer-assisted refinement based on a larger 54-uniform family yields ternary epsilon-synchronization strings for every epsilon > 226/227.
  The previous four-symbol construction uses a ternary square-free backbone to exclude short repetitions and a fourth symbol to carry long-range synchronization marks. Our main technical contribution is a local-entropy transfer theorem: every square-free block-local source with positive conditional min-entropy supports synchronization strings with a fixed gap. We instantiate this theorem using occurrence-wise branching in a Brinkhuis family. Every outcome remains ternary and square-free, while every long interval retains linear conditional min-entropy after all choices outside it are exposed. A deletion-ball estimate converts this entropy into anti-concentration for high longest common subsequences, and an asymmetric Lovasz Local Lemma enforces all interval constraints simultaneously. The same framework also yields exponentially many valid words, a Las Vegas polynomial-time construction, synchronization circles, and synchronization within a class of extremal square-free words.

</details>


<div id='cs.GR'></div>

# cs.GR [[Back]](#toc)

### [283] [ReRoom: Blending Virtual and Physical Contexts for In Situ Room Planning in Mixed Reality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03596)
*Hongliang Yang,Yanjing Xu,Anhang Zhang,Hui Ye,Pengfei Xu*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出 ReRoom，一种用于真实房间内现场（in situ）布局创作的混合现实系统，通过空间注册的虚拟房间代理将布局编辑与物理环境结合，支持直接操控和语言两种方式迭代细化布局，并使用技能引导的布局智能体在非矩形房间中生成高质量布局。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 规划真实居住空间本质上是现场创作过程：用户需要在真实尺度下评估候选布局、细化设计意图，并把已接受的决策延续到后续迭代中。然而现有方法要么将布局编辑与物理房间分离，要么对现场评估和细化整体房间方案的支持有限，缺乏能够保持物理上下文、支持连续迭代的创作工具。

Method: ReRoom 通过一个与目标房间空间注册的虚拟房间代理共享布局状态，使交互与布局生成始终锚定在物理场景中。用户可通过直接操控或自然语言细化当前方案，并保留已接受的位置，使每次生成更新延续同一演进中的设计。为兼顾布局质量与生成效率，ReRoom 采用技能引导的布局智能体：其房间布局设计技能将三条由室内设计准则综合提炼的原则操作化，并以扫描房间的归一化表示和可复用的几何检查作为落地基础。

Result: 评估表明，ReRoom 能为非矩形房间生成高质量布局，且其现场工作流相比同等条件下的离站式（off-site）VR 工作流显著改善了房间规划体验。

Conclusion: ReRoom 验证了将布局生成与物理上下文深度融合的现场创作范式的有效性：它不仅在非矩形房间上保持布局质量，还通过保持空间锚定与设计连续性提升了用户体验，说明混合现实现场流程是房间布局创作的可行方案。代码将在论文被接收后开源。

Abstract: Planning a real domestic space is an in situ authoring process: users evaluate candidate layouts at true scale, refine their intent, and carry accepted decisions into later iterations. Existing approaches either separate layout editing from the physical room or provide limited support for evaluating and refining whole-room proposals in situ. We present ReRoom, a mixed-reality system for in situ room-layout authoring. ReRoom presents a shared layout state through a virtual room proxy spatially registered to the target room, allowing interaction and layout generation to remain grounded in the physical context. Users refine the current proposal through direct manipulation or language and preserve accepted placements, allowing each generated update to continue the same evolving design. To balance layout quality with generation efficiency, ReRoom uses a skill-guided layout agent whose room-layout design skill operationalizes three principles that we formulate by synthesizing established interior-design guidance for real-room layout generation. The skill grounds these principles in a normalized representation of the scanned room and reusable geometric checks. Evaluations show that ReRoom produces high-quality layouts for non-rectangular rooms, while its in situ workflow improves the room-planning experience over an otherwise equivalent off-site VR workflow. Code will be released upon acceptance of the paper.

</details>


### [284] [TileGS: Tile-Local Depth Binning for Gaussian Splatting Rasterization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03613)
*Wei Tan,Matias Turkulainen,Lauri Ilola,Hamed Rezazadegan Tavakoli,Juho Kannala*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: TileGS 提出了一种对 3D 高斯泼溅（3DGS）的瓦片局部重组织方法，将每个长瓦片范围切分为一系列更短的深度局部范围并以前后次序栅格化，以减少光栅化遍历开销和几何属性流量。在桌面与笔记本 Ada GPU 的 9 场景基准上，其默认 No-GW 变体相比 gsplat 实现了平均 1.44 倍光栅化内核加速、4090 上 1.069 倍和 RTX 1000 Ada 上 1.094 倍的端到端帧加速，且输出指标与 gsplat 数值噪声级一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时 3D Gaussian Splatting（3DGS）虽能实现高渲染质量，但标准光栅化仍遍历全局排序的瓦片流，导致每个瓦片产生很长的范围并带来沉重的几何属性内存流量，成为渲染效率瓶颈。

Method: TileGS 将每个长瓦片范围重组织为一系列更短的深度局部范围，并以前到后的顺序进行光栅化；当粗粒度排序不足以匹配基线合成结果时，采用选择性修复（selective repair）机制。

Result: 在 9 场景基准上，默认 No-GW（无几何写回）变体在 RTX 4090 上平均获得 1.44 倍光栅化内核加速，端到端帧加速在 RTX 4090 上为 1.069 倍、在 RTX 1000 Ada 上为 1.094 倍；输出与 gsplat 一致（|ΔPSNR|<0.001 dB、|ΔSSIM|<0.001、|ΔLPIPS|<0.001）。Nsight Compute 剖析显示 TileGS 在更低 SM 吞吐、更低active-warp占用率和更高 DRAM 流量下仍更快，总 SASS 线程指令减少 1.26 倍；几何属性占剩余内存压力约 85.8% 的栅格化流量与 88.6% 的额外扇区。

Conclusion: 性能计数器共同表明：TileGS 的加速来自减少有效栅格化遍历工作量，而非降低字节量、改善合并访问、提高占用率或直接减少测得的 warp 发散；为 3DGS 光栅化优化提供了以遍历工作削减为核心的新思路。

Abstract: Real-time 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) achieves high rendering quality, but standard rasterization still traverses a globally sorted tile stream that creates long per-tile ranges and heavy geometry-attribute traffic. We present TileGS, a tile-local reorganization of Gaussian splatting. TileGS turns each long tile range into a sequence of shorter depth-local ranges, rasterizes those ranges in front-to-back order, and applies selective repair where coarse ordering is insufficient to match baseline compositing. Across a 9-scene benchmark on desktop and laptop Ada GPUs, our default No-GW (No Geometry-Write) variant delivers a mean 1.44x raster-kernel speedup on RTX 4090 and mean end-to-end frame speedups of 1.069x on RTX 4090 and 1.094x on RTX 1000 Ada over gsplat--a widely used optimized open-source 3DGS implementation--while matching the gsplat output up to numerical noise (|Delta PSNR| < 0.001 dB, |Delta SSIM| < 0.001, |Delta LPIPS| < 0.001). Full-suite RTX 4090 Nsight Compute profiling reveals TileGS is faster despite lower SM throughput, lower active-warp occupancy, and higher DRAM traffic, while total SASS thread instructions fall by 1.26x. Source-attributed profiling confirms that geometry attributes dominate the remaining memory pressure (85.8% of total raster traffic and 88.6% of excess sectors). Together, these counters support the interpretation that TileGS improves raster performance by reducing effective raster traversal work, rather than by reducing byte volume, improving coalescing, increasing occupancy, or directly reducing measured warp divergence.

</details>


### [285] [Reparametrizing 3D Gaussian Splatting for Real-Time Palette-based Color and Luminance Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03897)
*Cheng-Kang Ted Chao,Yotam Gingold*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出一个面向3D高斯泼溅(3DGS)的实时交互式颜色编辑框架，通过对预训练3DGS的球谐函数重新参数化来编码视图相关的调色板权重，并利用图像空间稀疏性损失联合求解权重与调色板颜色，实现基于调色板的重新着色、每个调色板的色调曲线亮度调节及像素级颜色约束。亮度编辑被建模为沿消色差轴的逐像素权重偏移，即逐像素的调色板感知亮度编辑。该视图空间公式克服了原始图元空间方法中alpha混合破坏逐高斯稀疏性、导致编辑溢出的局限，并通过迭代重加权最小二乘与阻尼块坐标下降在几十毫秒内完成编辑，且可高效烘焙回标准3DGS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专业色彩编辑要求对颜色（色相与饱和度）和亮度进行精确且独立的控制，而现有的3DGS调色板方法存在编辑溢出、无法独立控制亮度且缺乏视图一致性的问题。

Method: 对预训练的普通3DGS的球谐函数进行重新参数化以编码视图相关的调色板权重；通过基于图像空间稀疏性的损失同时求解权重和调色板颜色；将亮度编辑实现为沿消色差轴的逐像素权重偏移（等价于逐像素的调色板感知亮度编辑），并耦合色调曲线与调色板偏移，采用迭代重加权最小二乘和阻尼块坐标下降在视图空间稀疏性下求解；最终可将表示高效烘焙回普通3DGS。

Result: 相比先前的基于调色板的3DGS方法，实现了更稀疏、更局部的编辑，同时实现了每个调色板颜色的独立亮度控制以及视图一致的像素级约束，编辑运行耗时在几十毫秒量级。

Conclusion: 该视图空间框架有效解决了先前图元空间方法中alpha混合破坏稀疏性导致编辑溢出的核心局限，以实时性能提供了此前3DGS所不具备的独立亮度控制与像素级约束能力，同时保持与标准查看器的兼容性。

Abstract: Professional color editing requires precise control over both color (hue and saturation) and lightness, ideally through separate, independent controls. We present a real-time interactive color editing framework for 3D Gaussian Splatting that supports palette-based recoloring, per-palette tone curves for color-aware luminance adjustment, and pixel-level color constraints. Rather than training a new representation from scratch, we reparameterize the spherical harmonics of a pretrained vanilla 3DGS to encode view-dependent palette weights. We simultaneously solve for weights and palette colors via a loss based on image-space sparsity. Luminance editing is realized as a per-pixel weight shift along the achromatic axis, which we show is equivalent to a per-pixel palette-aware luminance edit. This view-space formulation addresses a core limitation of prior primitive-space methods, where alpha-blending breaks per-Gaussian sparsity and causes edits to bleed into unintended regions. Our edits run in tens of milliseconds via an iteratively reweighted least squares and damped block-coordinate descent that couples tone curves and palette shifts under view-space sparsity. Our representation can be efficiently baked back into a vanilla 3DGS, preserving compatibility with standard viewers. We demonstrate sparser, more localized edits than prior palette-based 3DGS methods, while enabling independent luminance control per palette color and view-consistent pixel-level constraints, capabilities previously unavailable for 3DGS.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [286] [Structure and Implementation of New Practical English Textbooks Driven by Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02981)
*Ya Wang,Lei Zhang,Xueguang Yang,Bo Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种由人工智能驱动的实用英语教材，采用五层架构（知识图谱、学习者画像、任务生成、反馈编排、教师端治理），经186名非英语专业本科生8周教学实验验证，相较静态数字教材可将单元完成准确率从72.4%提升至84.9%，口语任务均分提高10.8分，教师批改时间降低31.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工智能正在将应用英语教材从固定的纸质序列转变为能够诊断学习者、推荐任务并提供形成性反馈的自适应学习系统，因此需要研究新型AI驱动英语教材的结构与具体应用方式。

Method: 提出一个由AI驱动的实用英语教材五层架构，包括：知识图谱层、学习者画像层、任务生成层、反馈编排层和教师端治理层；并构建原型系统，在186名非英语专业本科生中开展为期8周的教学对照实验（与传统静态数字教材对比）。

Result: 与静态数字教材相比，该系统的单元完成准确率由72.4%提升至84.9%，口语任务平均分提高10.8分，教师批改时间减少31.6%。

Conclusion: AI驱动的教材能够在保持课程稳定性（延续统一课程体系）的同时，提供个性化学习路径、丰富的练习材料和可追溯的课堂数据，实现结构化的自适应英语教学。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence is changing the form of applied English materials from fixed paper sequences to adaptive learning systems that can diagnose learners, recommend tasks, and provide formative feedback. This paper studies the structure and application of a new practical English textbook driven by artificial intelligence. A five-layer architecture is proposed: knowledge mapping, learner profiling, task generation, feedback orchestration, and teacher-side governance. A prototype was tested on 186 non-English-major undergraduates for eight weeks of teaching. Compared with a static digital textbook, the proposed system increased the unit completion accuracy from 72.4% to 84.9%, raised the average score for speaking tasks by 10.8 points, and reduced the teacher's correction time by 31.6%. Therefore, an AI-driven textbook can maintain the stability of the curriculum while providing personalised learning paths, rich practice materials and traceable classroom data.

</details>


### [287] [MasterControl Seventeen Every Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03209)
*MasterControl AI Lab*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种受治理（governed）的企业分析方案：由语言模型仅负责解释用户问题，而由确定性策略从预批准的分析程序中选取并执行，同时返回结果与证据。该受限方式在关系运算、聚合、比较、窗口、排序和相似度等定义的分析类别内仍具表达力，并因固定的语义、策略、数据和执行规则而具备可复现性。实验显示，在440次运行中，3个8B模型进行运行时SQL生成与工具选择均未能完整满足答案+证据契约（330次运行时规划全部失败），而策略执行式分析器在110次中全部成功（110/110）。作者强调这是特定配置下的结果，并非否认运行时智能体在其他设计下不可行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业分析场景对准确性、可审计性和可复现性要求很高，纯粹的运行时规划/代码生成型智能体难以稳定满足“答案+证据”双重契约，因此需要探索一种受治理、可回放的分析范式，即将解释意图与执行决策分离。

Method: 采用“受限推理+确定性执行”的双层架构：语言模型只解释问题意图；确定性策略基于预批准的分析程序进行选择与执行。分析类别涵盖关系运算、聚合、比较、窗口、排名和相似度，并通过固定的语义、策略、数据与执行规则保证结果可重放。实验中对比三种8B模型的运行时SQL生成与工具选择，以及Qwen3-8B仅解释意图、由策略执行预批准程序的方案。

Result: 在440次运行中，330次运行时规划（runtime planning）尝试无一能在所有测试数据集上同时满足答案与证据的完整契约；而策略执行式分析器在110次运行中全部成功（110/110），展示了该治理方案在契约满足率上的显著优势。

Conclusion: 作者认为该结果表明：在特定配置下，受治理的“意图解释+预批准程序执行”方案能达到完全可复现且满足答案-证据契约的效果，优于基于相同规模模型的运行时规划方案；同时明确指出该结论是配置相关的结果，不能据此推断运行时智能体在其他设计下必然失败。

Abstract: We study a governed approach to enterprise analytics: a language model interprets the question, while deterministic policy selects and runs a pre-approved analytical program that returns both results and evidence. We show that this restriction can remain expressive within a defined analytical class, using relational operations plus aggregation, comparison, windows, ranking, and similarity. Fixed meaning, policy, data, and execution rules also make results replayable. Across 440 runs, three 8B models generated SQL and selected tools at runtime, while Qwen3-8B interpreted intent only and policy executed the approved program. None of 330 runtime-planning episodes matched the full answer-and-evidence contract across all test datasets; the policy-executed analyzer matched 110 of 110. This is a configuration-specific result, not evidence that runtime agents cannot succeed under other designs.

</details>


### [288] [Speculative Macro Commit for Faster Tool-Using Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03236)
*Zeyu Liu,Souvik Kundu,Peter A. Beerel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Speculative Macro Commit (SMC)，一种用于双层智能体系统的运行时机制：快速投机草稿模型在隔离环境快照上持续预测并预执行未来动作链，SMC 从训练轨迹中挖掘可复用的多步动作骨架存入宏库，当权威 Actor 模型的下一步工具调用与草稿预测的首个动作匹配时，将剩余的预执行步骤及其观测一次性提交到官方轨迹中，从而在不损失准确率的前提下大幅降低端到端延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具使用的 LLM 智能体不仅消耗模型推理时间，还会因串行的"动作-观测"循环（每次工具调用、环境转移和观测）产生大量墙钟时间开销，导致后续决策被延迟；现有单步投机动作（Speculative Actions）的加速效果有限，难以充分复用多步投机执行来降低智能体延迟。

Method: SMC 采用双层智能体系统：(1) 大型权威 Actor 模型生成官方轨迹，小型快速投机 Drafter 模型在隔离的环境快照上持续预测并执行未来的动作链；(2) 从训练轨迹中挖掘重复出现的多步动作骨架，构建宏库（macro library），用于在运行时匹配 Drafter 预测的动作链；(3) 当 Actor 的下一步工具调用与草稿预测的第一个动作匹配时，SMC 将剩余的预执行草稿步骤连同观测结果一起提交到官方轨迹中。

Result: 以 Qwen3.5-27B INT4 作为权威 Actor、Qwen3.5-4B 作为投机 Drafter：在 τ²-Bench Telecom 子集上，SMC 在保持与串行智能体相当的总体准确率的同时，相比 Speculative Actions (SA) 基线延迟降低 10.23%，相比串行执行延迟降低 18.59%；在 AppWorld 上，相比 SA 基线墙钟时间降低 7.7%，相比串行执行降低 44.9%，但任务完成率有小幅下降。

Conclusion: SMC 提供了一种实用的方式来复用多步投机执行，在单步投机动作之外进一步减少智能体延迟，并且代码已公开。其总体效果是以接近无损的准确率换取显著的墙钟时间缩减，超越单步投机基线。

Abstract: Tool-using LLM agents spend wall-clock time not only on model inference but also in serial action--observation turns, where each tool call, environment transition, and observation can delay subsequent decisions. We introduce \textbf{Speculative Macro Commit} (SMC), a runtime mechanism for a two-tier agent system: a large authoritative actor model produces the official trajectory, while a faster speculative drafter model continuously predicts and executes future action chains on an isolated environment snapshot. SMC mines recurring multi-action skeletons from training traces and stores them in a macro library used to match against action chains predicted by the drafter at runtime. When the actor's next tool call matches the first drafted action, SMC commits the remaining pre-executed draft steps, together with their observations, to the official trajectory. Using Qwen3.5-27B INT4 as the authoritative actor model and Qwen3.5-4B as the speculative drafter model, SMC matches the sequential agent's overall accuracy while reducing latency by 10.23\% over the Speculative Actions (SA) baseline and 18.59\% over sequential execution on the $τ^2$-Bench Telecom subset. On AppWorld, SMC reduces wall time by 7.7\% over SA baseline and 44.9\% over sequential execution, with a small reduction in task completion. Overall, SMC provides a practical way to reuse multi-step speculative execution and reduce agent latency beyond single-step speculative actions. Our code is publicly available \href{https://github.com/zeyuliu1037/speculative-macro-commit}{\textcolor{magenta}{here}}.

</details>


### [289] [Fresh Memory, Stale Plans: Dependency-Scoped Validation for Distributed LLM-Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03340)
*Evan Chen,Shiqiang Wang,Christopher G. Brinton*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出并验证了 PlanFence，一种用于分布式 LLM 智能体团队的依赖范围动作验证协议，以解决“陈旧计划执行”问题——即共享状态虽已新鲜，但授权某动作的计划可能已失效。在 30 个受控实时工作流中，PlanFence 全部任务无无效动作完成，而纯新鲜度检查执行器在每个任务中都会执行过时计划；受控重放进一步揭示了两个条件性边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式 LLM 智能体团队即使读取到最新的共享事实，仍可能基于过时的计划执行动作。例如规划者依据需求 r3 生成动作，另一智能体提交了 r4，执行者收到 r4 却未替换依据 r3 制定的计划。作者将此定义为“陈旧计划执行”：状态的新鲜度并不能保证授权动作的计划仍然有效，现有基于状态新鲜度的机制不足以保障动作安全。

Method: 提出 PlanFence 协议：计划需引用其使用的确切公共记录（依赖清单），执行者仅验证那些可能影响待执行外部动作的记录，一旦验证不完整则触发重新规划或阻止执行。通过 30 个带计划后修订的受控实时工作流与受控重放实验，与仅做新鲜度检查的执行器进行对比评估。

Result: 在 30 个受控实时工作流中，纯新鲜度执行器在每个任务中都依据过时计划动作，而 PlanFence 全部完成且无无效动作。受控重放揭示两个条件性边界：低变更率下主动同步的协调停滞更低；随着变更率增长，PlanFence 避免重复的更新路径协调；随着共享键空间增大，PlanFence 避免验证无关状态。这些是受控的安全性验证与系统成本结果，而非通用的任务准确率提升。

Conclusion: PlanFence 通过依赖作用域的动作验证，能有效消除陈旧计划执行带来的无效外部动作，相较新鲜度检查具有明确的安全优势；其成本优势具有条件性，取决于变更率与共享键空间规模。作者强调该结论局限于受控安全性与系统成本考量，不宜过度泛化为通用任务精度收益。

Abstract: Distributed LLM-agent teams can read the latest shared facts and still act on an obsolete plan. A planner may derive an action from requirement $r_3$, another agent may commit $r_4$, and an executor may receive $r_4$ without replacing the plan derived from $r_3$. We call this \emph{stale-plan execution}: state freshness does not establish that the plan authorizing an action remains valid. We introduce PlanFence, a dependency-scoped action-validation protocol. Plans cite the exact public records they used, and an executor validates only the records that can affect the pending external action, replanning once or blocking when validation is incomplete. In 30 controlled live workflows with a post-plan revision, a freshness-only executor acts on the obsolete plan in every task, whereas PlanFence completes all tasks without an invalid action. Controlled replay reveals two conditional boundaries: proactive synchronization yields lower coordination stall at low churn, while PlanFence avoids repeated update-path coordination as churn grows and avoids validating unrelated state as the shared keyspace grows. These are controlled safety and systems-cost results, not general task-accuracy gains.

</details>


### [290] [A Prompt-Engineering Approach to Develop Scalable, Flexible, and Real-Time Hybrid Micro-Level Personalization in a General Purpose AI Teaching Assistant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03402)
*Saptarshi Basu,Sandeep Kakar,Ashok Goel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于提示工程（prompt engineering）的框架，用于个性化定制基于通用大语言模型（LLM）和检索增强生成（RAG）的AI教学助手（如Jill Watson），使其能够跨学科和课程进行自适应响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由大语言模型驱动的AI教学助手虽能提供可扩展的教育支持，但通常缺乏个性化能力，难以适应不同学习者的个体差异。

Method: 框架使用六个学习者维度（自我评估、抽象偏好、冗长偏好、感知取向、信息处理风格和理解水平）生成96种学习者画像；同时利用Bloom分类法在交互层面估计学生问题的认知复杂度；将学习者属性与认知评估编码为结构化提示以调节LLM输出，无需重新训练模型。评估结合NLP指标实验和五人人类研究。

Result: 实验结果显示，不同个性化条件下响应风格与结构存在可感知差异，统计分析识别出与可测量响应变化相关的学习者属性，为提示式个性化支持自适应行为提供了初步证据。

Conclusion: 提示工程驱动的个性化能够在不重新训练模型的情况下为LLM驱动的教育代理带来自适应行为，为AI教学助手的跨学科个性化提供了可行且可扩展的途径。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) teaching assistants powered by large language models (LLMs) offer scalable educational support but often provide limited personalization. This study presents a prompt-engineering-based framework for personalizing general-purpose LLM/RAG-based AI teaching assistants such as Jill Watson across academic disciplines and courses. The framework adapts responses using six learner-specific dimensions: self-assessment, abstraction preference, verbosity preference, perceptual orientation, information processing style, and level of understanding, yielding 96 distinct learner profiles. Student queries are additionally analyzed using Bloom's Taxonomy to estimate cognitive complexity at the interaction level. Learner attributes and cognitive assessments are encoded in structured prompts that condition the LLM without requiring model retraining. The framework is evaluated through experiments using NLP metrics and a human study with five participants. Results show perceived differences in response style and structure across personalization conditions, with statistical analyses identifying learner attributes associated with measurable response changes. These findings provide preliminary evidence that prompt-based personalization can support adaptive behavior in LLM-powered educational agents.

</details>


### [291] [Caught in the Story: Narrative Captivity in Multi-turn LLMs Conversation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03407)
*Yuhe Wu,Guangyu Wang,Yujie Chen,Jiatong Zhang,Yuran Chen,Yutong Zhang,Xiyin Cheng,Wenpeng Cao,Zhuang Liu,Guang Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"叙事俘获"（narrative captivity）这一失败模式：在多轮道德咨询中，大语言模型将被单方叙述而未被反驳的片面陈述视为完整信息，并倾向于认同叙述者的解释而不再寻求缺失视角。作者构建了涵盖六个道德维度、包含5,078个冲突场景的基准，在17个LLM上验证表明该现象普遍存在：多轮叙述下的终局判断平均比单轮基线偏移25个百分点。阶段级分析指出偏好优化（preference optimization）是主要诱因，四种推理时策略仅能部分缓解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中人们越来越多地向LLM寻求日常道德建议，而真实的人际道德冲突往往以一方自我辩护的片面叙述展开，可跨越多个回合并造成信息不对称。此前研究多通过单轮判断或施压式反驳来考察该场景，假设与实际咨询方式不符，且未弄清"仅凭叙述、无需明确对立立场"是否就能在多轮道德咨询中改变模型判断。因此需要系统度量该失败模式。

Method: 作者提出"叙事俘获"这一失败模式，构建了包含5,078个人际冲突情景、跨越六个道德维度的基准来度量该现象；在17个LLM上进行评测，对比多轮叙述下的终局判断与匹配的单轮基线的偏移程度，并通过阶段级（stage-level）分析定位训练各环节的贡献，同时评测四种推理时（inference-time）缓解策略。

Result: 叙事俘获在17个LLM中普遍存在：多轮叙述使终局判断平均比单轮基线偏移25个百分点。阶段级分析将偏好优化识别为主要贡献因素；四种推理时缓解策略仅能提供部分缓解效果。

Conclusion: 叙事俘获是当前LLM道德建议系统中一个广泛且顽固的缺陷，偏好优化是其重要来源，现有推理时策略无法充分抵消。作者希望该工作能推动LLM在真实咨询场景中保持独立判断能力。

Abstract: People increasingly turn to large language models (LLMs) for everyday advice, making ethically charged interpersonal problems a practical moral-advisory context. Most prior work has studied this context through single-turn judgments or pressure-laden rebuttals, assumptions that poorly match how guidance is sought in real-world contexts. These assumptions leave unclear whether narration alone, without an explicit opposing position, can shift model judgments during multi-turn moral consultation.
